\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
The class \doxylink{classofeli_1_1_image_viewer}{Image\+Viewer} is a graphical user interface using the Qt6 framework (Qt\+Core, Qt\+Gui, Qt\+Widgets and Qt\+Print\+Support) to show an image with the active contour, to configure the parameters of the active contour and optionally the preprocessing step and to evaluate two segmentation results thanks to the evaluation window. It is based on a legacy Qt image-\/viewer example.
